```text
  db: Db;
  client: ScriptedClient;
  app: ReturnType<typeof createApp>;

const opened: Db[] = [];

function rig(): Rig {
  const db = openDatabase({ path: ':memory:' });
  migrate(db);
  opened.push(db);
  const client = scripted();
  return { db, client, app: createApp({ db, client, cassetteDir: '.tmp/fixtures' }) };
}

afterEach(() => {
  while (opened.length > 0) {
    const db = opened.pop();
    if (db !== undefined) closeDatabase(db);
  }
});
```

```text
 * 一位面试官的意图契约，连同它此刻的指纹与运行时指纹的对照结果。
 *
 * 契约是**声明式意图**：不在数据库里存全文，而是按同样的角色与预算重新算一遍。
 * 重算的意义在于对账——如果代码里的契约被人改过，重算出的指纹就和会话里存的对不上，
 * 界面会直接显示「契约漂移」。这比存一份可能过期的副本要诚实。
 */
export interface ContractView {
  id: string;
  version: number;
  /** 此刻用代码里的契约算出的指纹 */
  digest: string;
  /** 跑这场沙盘时落库的指纹；没跑过或旧会话可能是 null */
  storedDigest: string | null;
  /** 指纹与版本都对得上，才叫「运行时一致」 */
  verified: boolean;
  contract: IntentContract;
  /** 这位面试官实际拿到的 system prompt，可逐字对照契约 */
  systemPrompt: string;
}

function metaContracts(meta: Record<string, unknown>): StoredContract[] {
  const value = meta['contracts'];
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  const out: StoredContract[] = [];
  for (const item of value) {
    if (item === null || typeof item !== 'object') continue;
    const raw = item as Record<string, unknown>;
    const id = raw['id'];
    const digest = raw['digest'];
    if (typeof id !== 'string' || typeof digest !== 'string') continue;
    const version = raw['version'];
    const stored: StoredContract = { id, version: typeof version === 'number' ? version : 1, digest };
    const contract = raw['contract'];
    if (contract !== null && typeof contract === 'object') stored.contract = co
…（此处省略 1917 字符，原始长度 3117）
```

```text
/**
 * 测试用的"脚本化模型"：不发网络请求，按调用次序返回预先准备好的文本。
 *
 * 为什么值得单独存在：多 Agent 流水线的测试痛点是"每个 Agent 都要一份假数据"，
 * 用脚本化客户端之后，测试写的是"第 1 次调用返回什么"，而不是"怎么 mock fetch"。
 * 它同时也是离线演示的录制工具——record 模式包上它，就能在没有任何 API Key 的机器上
 * 生成一整套完整卡带。
 */

import type { LlmClient, LlmRequest, LlmStreamEvent } from '../src/llm/types.ts';

export type ScriptedHandler = (req: LlmRequest, index: number) => string | Error;

export interface ScriptedClient extends LlmClient {
  readonly calls: LlmRequest[];
  /** 每次调用的返回值，便于断言"到底调了几次、按什么顺序" */
  countPurpose(purpose: string): number;
}

const USAGE = { inputTokens: 10, outputTokens: 20, cacheReadTokens: 0, reasoningTokens: 0 };

export function createScriptedClient(handler: ScriptedHandler): ScriptedClient {
  const calls: LlmRequest[] = [];
  function take(req: LlmRequest): string {
    calls.push(req);
    const out = handler(req, calls.length - 1);
    if (out instanceof Error) throw out;
    return out;
  }
  const client: LlmClient = {
    mode: 'live',
    async complete(req: LlmRequest) {
      const text = take(req);
      return { text, usage: USAGE, digest: 'scripted-' + String(calls.length), replayed: false, elapsedMs: 0 };
    },
    async *stream(req: LlmReques
```

```text
const STYLE_IN_SYSTEM = ['保守', '常规', '敢改'];

function styleOf(system: string): string {
  for (let i = 0; i < STYLE_IN_SYSTEM.length; i += 1) {
    if (system.indexOf('用户选的档位：' + STYLE_IN_SYSTEM[i]) >= 0) {
      return ['conservative', 'normal', 'bold'][i]!;
    }
  }
  return 'normal';
}

function scripted(): ScriptedClient {
  return createScriptedClient((req) => {
    if (req.purpose === 'extract') return EXTRACT;
    if (req.purpose === 'probe') {
      return JSON.stringify({
        probes: [{ claimId: 'C1', question: '这个 0.8s 是怎么测出来的？', quote: QUOTE, rationale: '测量方法最容易被追问' }],
      });
    }
    if (req.purpose === 'answer') return ANSWER;
    if (req.purpose === 'verdict') return VERDICT;
    if (req.purpose === 'suggest') {
      const after = AFTER_BY_STYLE[styleOf(req.system)] ?? AFTER_BY_STYLE['normal']!;
      // problem / why 都是 schema 的必填项：少一个整条建议会被判为格式不符，直接变成一条错误
      return JSON.stringify({
        suggestions: [{ claimId: 'C1', problem: '说了结果但没说清测试条件', before: QUOTE, after, why: '补上测量口径' }],
      });
    }
```

```text
## 4.4 离线回放：没有 Key 也能看到完整效果

### 4.4.1 卡带机制

回放模式下，每一次模型调用的「问题」被哈希成一个 24 位指纹，**答案**就存在以该指纹命名的 JSON 文件里：

```
fixtures/llm/
├── 0a3f...c2.json    extract 阶段的回答
├── 1b7e...d9.json    probe 阶段某位面试官的回答
└── ...               共 48 盘
```

指纹只对请求的五项内容做哈希（system / messages / temperature / maxTokens / json），
因此**改了提示词，指纹就变，旧卡带自然失效并响亮报错**。这既是一个约束，也是一道门禁（4.4.3）。

### 4.4.2 48 盘卡带覆盖了什么

| 阶段 | 卡带数 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| extract | 1 | 抽主张 |
| jd-extract / jd-merge / jd-match | 各 1 | 读一遍内置示例 JD：抽要求 → 归并 → 与主张逐条对照 |
| probe | 24 | 3 位面试官 × 三档追问强度，逐档一套预算 |
| answer | 10 | 10 条追问各一次作答 |
| verdict | 5 | 5 条主张各一次判定 |
| suggest | 4 | 4 条需要改写的建议（「站得住」的那条跳过） |
| counter | 1 | 一轮「轮到你了」的合成反问 |

跑完一场离线演示的权威数字（`.tmp/doc-stats2.mts` 直接调 `runAndPersist` 实测，常规档）：

| 指标 | 值 |
| --- | --- |
| 耗时 | 46 ms |
| 轮数 / 主张 / 追问 / 判定 / 建议 | 3 / 5 / 10 / 5 / 4 |
| 被锚定机制拦下的主张 | 1 |
| 判定分布 | 站得住 1 · 表述过度 1 · 证据缺失 2 · 无法回答 1 |
| token | 输入 27,050 · 输出 4,122 · 思考 724（合计 31,172） |
| 反问 | 4 句（依据 JD 2 · 依据简历原话 2） |
| 每位面试官追问数 | tech 4 · hr 3 · hiring_manager 3 |
| 岗位要求对照 | 7 条要求：答得上 1 · 答得含糊 3 · 简历里没写 3 |
| 模型调用 | 33 次，回放 33 
…（此处省略 416 字符，原始长度 1616）
```

<details><summary>✅ 执行结果（原始 1569 字符，已折叠）</summary>

```text
282: ### 2.6.2 建议阶段的四道闸门
283: 
284: 改写建议是最容易被模型「发挥」的环节——它会顺手帮你加一个更漂亮的数字。
285: 因此 `suggest.ts` 设了四道闸门，任何一道不过，这条建议被拒绝并记录原因：
286: 
287: | 闸门 | 检查 | 拒绝理由 |
288: | --- | --- | --- |
289: | 是否需要建议 | 判定为「站得住」的主张直接跳过（不调用模型、不消耗预算） | 判定为「站得住」，不需要改写 |
290: | 改前原文可定位 | `before` 必须能在简历里锚定到 | 建议里的「改前原文」在简历中定位不到，无法应用 |
291: | 不许引入新事实 | `after` 里的数字必须是简历里已有的数字 | 建议里出现了简历原文中不存在的数字，属于新增事实 |
292: | 必须真的改 | `after` 不能为空，也不能等于 `before` | 建议改写后与原文相同，等于没改 |
293: 
294: 第二道闸门的具体实现是 `newNumbersIn(after, resumeText)`：用正则 `/[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?/g` 抠出建议里的所有数字，
295: 逐个检查是否在简历原文的数字集合里出现过；只要有一个新的，整条建议作废。
296: 
297: 这条规则的效果可以在离线演示里直接看到：C2 的改写建议里出现的 `800` 与 `5000` 都来自简历原文，
298: 模型没有机会把「QPS 5000」改成「QPS 8000」。
299: 
300: ### 2.6.3 归属由调用方决定
301: 
302: 建议的归属（这条建议属于哪条主张）**一律由调用方传入**，模型自报的 `claimId` 不看。
303: 这一条在 live 实测里救过一次命：schema 曾把 `claimId` 列为必填，而提示词从没要求模型输出它，
304: 于是 9 条建议全部因「claimId: Required」被拒；离线演示恰好写了这个字段，把矛盾完整掩盖。
305: 修复后 `claimId` 改为 optional，并补了「模型不返回 claimId 也能出建议」的回归测试。
306: 
307: 这个 bug 的教训写在 `findings.md` F23，也是本文档第 5.6 节要展开的「给模型输出造夹具的唯一真相是提示词里的输出要求」。
308: 
309: ## 2.7 过程可视化：流式分片是观感，事件日志才是真相
310: 
311: 赛题的 UX 评分项明确点名了「流式、思考可视化」。本项目的处理方式是一条明确的分界：
312: 
313: | | 流式分片 | 阶段事件 |
314: | --- | --- | --- |
315:
```

<details><summary>✅ 执行结果（原始 1483 字符，已折叠）</summary>

```text
344: 包装层把它改写成 `for await (inner.stream(req))`，把 `delta` 与 `reasoning` 分片送进 sink，最终结果取自 `done` 事件。
345: 分片通过 SSE 的 `event: stream` 送出，**不带 id、不占 seq、不进事件表**。
346: 
347: 理由写在 ADR-0004 里：分片每秒几十片，落库会让回放变慢、日志变吵，而分片拼出来的文本并不比 done 事件更可信。
348: **能断线续传的只有阶段事件——这是「可复现」与「好看」之间的一条明确分界。**
349: 
350: 顺带一个工程细节：分片携带的 `channel` 字段（例如 `answer:P1`、`verdict:C3`）**刻意不参与卡带指纹**，
351: 因为指纹只对请求做哈希（system / messages / temperature / maxTokens / json）。
352: 这条设计让「加流式」这件事不需要重录任何一盘离线卡带。
353: 
354: ### 2.7.1 思考分片：接上会思考的模型，界面上多一行斜体
355: 
356: 思考（`reasoning`）与正文（`delta`）是两个独立通道，用分片上的 `kind` 区分；界面渲染成两行——
357: 斜体那行是它正在「想」的，正下方那行是它要「说」的。要让这一行真的出现，需要三件事：
358: 
359: | 环节 | 做法 | 为什么 |
360: | --- | --- | --- |
361: | 选谁思考 | 装配层 `withStageModels(inner, routes)`，按 `req.purpose` 换模型 | 判定才需要「停下来想」；追问一轮十几次调用，全交给思考模型会把一场从十几秒拉成几分钟（ADR-0005） |
362: | 换模型不发卡带 | `LlmRequest.model` 与 `channel` 一样不参与指纹 | 已录好的 56 盘演示卡带不必重录 |
363: | 没有 Key 也看得见 | 合成脚本把 `reasoning` 写进卡带（同一个通道） | 离线演示也要能证明「思考可视化」是产品能力，不是联网才有的彩蛋；卡带 `model` 固定 `scripted-demo`，一眼可辨 |
364: 
365: 实测（2026-10-26，示例简历，`CROSSEXAM_REASONING_MODEL=deepseek-reasoner` 只路由 verdict）：
366: 一场得到 **2,355 片思考分片 / 8 路 `verdict:*` 通道**，账本 `reasoningTokens` 大于 0，同一场正文分片 5,814 片，两条通道都不进事件表。
3
```

```text
369: ## 2.8 本章要点
370: 
371: - 意图控制不是写提示词，是**四层可判定的机制**：声明（契约）→ 校验（锚定）→ 记账（预算）→ 收敛（规则）。
372: - 每条提问必须锚定简历原文，这是「提问可证伪」的地基，也是幻觉提问被物理消灭的原因。
373: - 追问预算由代码记账而非模型数数，因此死循环在**设计上**不成立，而不是「我们尽量避免了」。
374: - 判定与建议都要过代码复核：模型想判「站得住」必须拿出证据；想改简历不许引入新数字。
375: - 契约不落库全文，只落指纹，读侧重算对账——一份真值 + 一个指纹，胜过两份迟早不同步的副本。
```

<details><summary>✅ 执行结果（原始 955 字符）</summary>

```text
1: # ADR-0016：改前改后对照要有上下文，而且只算一遍
2: 
3: - 状态：已采纳（2026-10-27，P11 的 B2）
4: - 相关：决策 D50 / D51、发现 F62 / F63、`packages/shared/src/revise.ts`、`packages/server/src/report.ts`、`packages/server/src/report-html.ts`、`packages/web/src/components/RevisionBar.tsx`
5: 
6: ## 背景
7: 
8: 采纳改写之后，简历被真的改过，用户需要一个能核对的东西：哪一句被改成了什么。
9: 报告（Markdown / 可打印 HTML / Word）与界面（采纳栏）都要能回答这个问题，而且必须给出**同一个答案**。
10: 
11: ## 决策
12: 
13: 1. **聚合只写一遍**。`sideBySide(resumeText, applied)` 产出若干行，报告与界面都读它；两侧都不许自己遍历 `applied` 拼结论。
14: 2. **摆上下文行**。改动之间没动过的原文也要进来，两侧相同，朝改动的方向截断（改动之前留尾巴、之后留开头）。
15: 3. **不做字符级 diff**。中文改写多为整句重写，字符级对齐会碎成无意义的片段。
16: 4. **只在真的采纳过之后出现**。没采纳就不叫「改前改后」；`applied` 为空时两份报告都跳过这一节，章节编号随之回退（复现方式仍然是六）。
17: 5. **界面默认收起**。它是核对时才看的东西，不该长期占着采纳栏的地方。
18: 
19: ## 取舍
20: 
21: - 两列不逐字符对齐，换来的是「读得懂」；要逐字对齐应该去看简历本体（右栏已经把改动段标绿）。
22: - 上下文长度固定 24 字（`SIDE_CONTEXT_CHARS`），超出即截断并加省略号；因此可能出现「上下文」行两侧一模一样的情况——这是刻意的：它标的是位置，不是内容。
23: - 报告里多一节意味着章节编号会随内容变（六七之间来回）。选「编号连续」而不是「编号固定、留个空号」，因为报告是给人读的。
```

</details>

<details><summary>✅ 执行结果（原始 780 字符）</summary>

```text
194: | D49 | 反问的回答必须**在界面上标注是沙盘模拟** | 每条反问都附了「对方大概会答」。这一栏很有用，也很危险：用户可能把它当成真实面试答案背出去。所以回答由沙盘里的求职者 Agent 代答，界面上直接写「沙盘模拟，不是真 HR 在答」，报告里也照写——宁可让这一节显得「只是练习」，也不能让人拿它去赌一场真面试；见 ADR-0015 |
195: | D50 | 「改前改后对照」的行由 `packages/shared/src/revise.ts` 的 `sideBySide` 产出，报告与界面都读它；**没采纳过就整节不出现**（章节编号跟着回退） | 同一份结论在报告与界面各算一遍，失败方式不是崩溃而是「两个都对但不一样」（F63，与 D47 同族）。「没采纳过」时那种「对照」只是把同一份原文抄两遍，摆出来会让人以为简历已经改过了；所以
196: | D51 | 对照表只摆**改动行 + 上下文行**，不做字符级 diff | 中文改写常常是整句重写，字符级 diff 会碎成一堆无意义的片段，反而看不出「改成了什么」。读者真正缺的信息是「这句改动落在简历的什么位置」，所以把改动之间没动过的原文也摆进来当上下文行（F62）。代价是左右两列不逐字符对齐——这是刻意的：它要服务的是「读得懂」，不是「逐字对齐」 |
197: | D52 | 技术文档**不再让每章强制另起一页** | 30 页是赛题硬上限，而 `h1.section { break-before: page }` 会把每章末尾那半页空白也算进预算：31 页里有一整页只有 230 个字符。改成章间留白（9mm）+ 一条细分隔线之后，同一份内容 31 → 28 页，余量从 0 变成 2 页。代价是章标题可能出现在页面中部——对一份要被检索、被打开展示的技术
```

```text
26: | 审阅 | **岗位要求对照**：贴了 JD 就先把它读成一张要求清单（逐份抽要求 → 跨份归并 → 与你的主张逐条对照），分成「答得上 / 答得含糊 / 简历里没写」三组；前两组会被拿去追问，一条追问同时挂 JD 原文片段与简历片段**两处锚点**，点任一处右栏就聚焦到那一段 |
27: | 组庭 | 五位预设面试官（技术 / HR / 业务主管 / 压力 / 领域专家），也可以在**面试官工作室**里自己配一位：一句话描述 → 补成意图契约，没有 API Key 就用本地规则生成；契约、指纹与渲染出的 System Prompt 都能摊开看 |
28: | 对抗 | 多面试官并发追问，你回答，它继续追问 |
29: | 判定 | 每条主张：站得住 / 表述过度 / 证据缺失 / 与原文矛盾 / 无法回答，并写明「为什么这样判」 |
30: | 反推 | 定位到原文片段的简历修改建议 |
31: | 短板 | 同一批判定换个角度：按主张类别聚合，先排欠得最多的那一类，再列出最该补的 3 处，点一下跳回简历原文 |
32: | 强度 | **追问强度**三档：温和 / 常规 / 挑刺。换的是预算（每主张 2 / 3 / 4 轮、全场 40 / 60 / 80 次）不是人设，档位随会话留档 |
33: | 补证据 | 任何一条主张上可以补一句真话当作新证据；采纳时它作为新的一行进简历，原文一个字不动 |
34: | 反问 | 跑完给你几句能反问 HR 的话，每句都必须锚回简历原话或 JD 原文，锚不回的当场丢弃；回答明确标注是沙盘模拟 |
35: | 改稿 | 勾选想采纳的建议 → **确认后自动改简历**，右栏直接换成改完的版本；改动段标绿，改不动的（引文在原简历里锚不住）会说明理由。点「并排看改动」还能逐条对照改前改后，并看到它们落在简历的什么位置 |
36: | 带走 | 报告三种形态：Markdown / 可打印 HTML（另存为 PDF）/ Word；改完的简历另外三种：`.txt` / 导出 PDF / 导出 Word |
37: | 模式 | 页面里**逐场**选运行模式：离线回放（默认，不联网）/ 联网直连 / 联网录制；模型名可下拉可手填，没配好就直接告诉你缺哪个环境变量 |
38: | 上手 | 第一次打开自动弹一次新手教程：四步用起来、三种模式对照、隐私口径、常见问题 |
39: | 变体 | 深色 / 浅色两套配色，头部一键切换并记住选择（浅色给白天与投影用） |
```

```text
30: 合计 13 项 + 1 个新大功能。
31: 
32: ## 2. 取材与合规：别人的东西哪些能用
33: 
34: 结论先给：**代码能用（看许可证），JD 文本不能批量抓，功能创意随便参考。**
35: 
36: ### 2.1 代码与开源项目
37: 
38: | 用法 | 能不能 | 条件 |
39: | --- | --- | --- |
40: | 引一个 MIT / Apache-2.0 / ISC 的库 | 能 | 保留版权声明；本仓库已有 unpdf、hono 等 |
41: | 读别人的开源实现找思路，自己重写 | 能 | 不构成复制；建议在 ADR 里写清「参考了谁的思路」 |
42: | 把别人的项目 fork / 换皮当参赛作品 | **不能** | 赛题红线：严禁直接使用已有开源项目包装参赛 |
43: | 抄一段 GPL / AGPL 代码进本仓库 | **不能** | 会把整个仓库变成 GPL，与 MIT 冲突 |
44: | 抄一段没写许可证的代码 | **不能** | 默认全保留版权，等于侵权 |
45: 
46: 参考对象（只读思路，不拷代码）：ResumeForge（静态事实台账）、各类 JD 匹配工具、ATS 关键词工具。
47: 共同点是「简历 → 关键词 → 打分」，我们要证明的恰好相反：**关键词匹配能告诉你缺什么词，
48: 但只有对抗式追问能告诉你哪句话经不起问。**
49: 
50: ### 2.2 JD 文本：BOSS 直聘这条路走不通，而且不该走
51: 
52: 实测（2026-10-26）：`https://www.zhipin.com/robots.txt` 对通用爬虫写了 `Disallow: /*?*`，
53: 而它的职位列表页全是带查询参数的 URL（`/web/geek/job?query=...&city=...`）。也就是说
54: **robots 协议下几乎没有可抓的职位页**。再加上用户协议普遍禁止批量抓取、平台有反爬与封禁机制，
55: 公开仓库里放一个「BOSS 爬虫」会同时带来三个问题：法律风险、封号风险、评委观感。
56: 
57: 所以本项目的取材口径是：
58: 
59: 1. **模板 JD 由我们自己写**：原创文案，按行业分组，不抄任何平台的原文（D1 扩容走这条路）。
60: 2. **用户自己的 JD 由用户上传**：他已经有权阅读的 JD，粘进产品里用，这是最干净的路。
61: 3. **开放数据集要逐个核许可证**：CC BY-NC / ND 一律不用（商用与改作受限）；找不到明确许可证就当不能用。
62: 4. **企业官网招聘页**：个别 robots 友好，但同样不做批量抓取——一次性的少量参考可以，不入库、不分发。
63: 
64:
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I need to design C1: 简历版本对比（改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变）.

Key idea: When a session has a revised resume (revised_resume table), the user can "改完再跑一遍" — start a new session using the revised text as the resume, linked to the parent session as a "version". Then compare verdicts claim-by-claim.

Design questions:
1. How to link versions? Add `parent_session_id` + `version` to session meta, or a new column. Given AGENTS.md/D44 rule: fields shown in UI must persist in business tables. So maybe add columns to session table: `parent_id TEXT`, `version INTEGER`, or store in meta_json. Hmm — D44 rule says "字段只要在界面上出现过，就必须落到业务表". meta_json is a business column though? Previous decisions used meta_json for difficulty/style. Let's check how `meta_json` is used for difficulty/style — from summary: "档位写 session.meta.difficulty". So meta is the established place for session-level knobs. But version parentage is a relationship, not a knob. I think cleaner: new table `session_version` or columns.

Actually simplest and most defensible: add `parent_session_id TEXT` and `version INTEGER` columns to session table via migrateV10. Then C1 comparison = list all sessions in the same version chain (by resume lineage), compare claim verdicts.

But claims are re-extracted each run, so C1..Cn are NOT the same claims across versions. Comparing claim-by-claim by id is wrong. Need a matching strategy: match claims across versions by their anchor quote / text similarity. That's the real intellectual content of C1: **不同版本的主张不是一一对应的**。

Good design: match by claim text normalization (stableStringify? no) — use a similarity: exact quote match first (normalized whitespace), then token overlap of claim text (Jaccard on bigrams of CJK chars?). Chinese text — use character bigrams. That's simple, deterministic, no deps.

So the comparison output: 
- 新增的主张（old 没有）
- 消失的主张（new 没有）
- 保留的主张：判定怎么变（improved / worsened / same）

That's a genuinely good feature and reuses shortfall/verdict labels.

Let me look at what exists: packages/shared/src/shortfall.ts, verdict kinds, domain.ts. Let me check the plan file for P11 C1 spec details maybe in the roadmap. It just says "简历版本对比（改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变）".

Before diving in, let me check the current state: is there any existing "版本" concept? Let me grep for "version" in shared/web.

Also I should check remaining plan: C1, C2, C3, then P12 (成长档案 + 一岗一版简历), P13, P14. That's a lot. Time budget: 功能冻结线 2026-11-08, now 2026-10-27. So ~12 days.

Let me start C1. First survey relevant files quickly.

Plan for C1:
1. shared/src/versions.ts — 纯函数：对两条 claim 列表做匹配 + 判定变化分类。
2. server: session 表加 parent_session_id / version 列（migrateV10）；createSession API 接受 parentSessionId（服务端校验父会话存在且有 revised resume，并把父会话的简历正文作为本次简历，或让前端传）。
3. server: GET /api/sessions/:id/compare?against=<id> 或 /api/versions? 返回对比结果。也许 GET /api/sessions/:id/compare 自动找父版本。
4. web: 报告与界面加「与上一版对比」。
5. e2e + 测试 + 文档。

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I have a clear picture. Let me plan C1: 简历版本对比（改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变）.

Design:
- Session A (first run) produces claims with verdicts and advice. User adopts revisions → revised_resume with applied text.
- C1: take the revised resume text (or the current resume text with applied revisions) and re-run a new session with the same JD and panel → then compare claim-by-claim verdicts.

Key design questions:
1. How to correlate claims across two sessions? Claims are numbered C1..Cn per session, regenerated by the model. So we need a matching key. Options: (a) by quote text anchored position — but the resume changed so positions shift; (b) by claim text similarity; (c) by category + keyword overlap. Best: lexical similarity on claim text with a threshold, plus anchor quote match. Let me design a pure function in shared: `alignClaims(a, b)` deterministic: first pass exact text match (normalized), second pass quote match, third pass token-overlap similarity ≥ threshold with greedy best-match. Unmatched are "gone"/"new".

2. Verdict comparison: for each matched pair, classify change: improved (unsupported/overstated/contradicts/unanswerable → solid), worsened (solid → something), unchanged, new, gone.

3. Where does this live? A server endpoint: POST /api/sessions/:id/rerun → creates a derived session with parent pointer, or better: GET /api/sessions/:id/versions — actually we need to store lineage. Simplest: session.meta.parentId + meta.derivedFrom. Then GET /api/compare?a=&b= returns a comparison view. And a UI: a "改完再跑一遍" button in RevisionBar that creates the new session with the revised text and runs it, then shows a comparison panel.

Given the scale, let me keep it focused:
- shared/src/compare.ts: pure functions for aligning claims + summarizing verdict deltas (testable, deterministic).
- server: session.meta.parentSessionId / lineage; POST /api/sessions/:id/derived (create new session seeded from revised resume + same JD/panel/difficulty/style); GET /api/sessions/:id/compare?against=<parentId> or /api/compare/:a/:b.
- web: a button + a comparison view (new panel or modal).
- report: a section in the report when the session has a parent.

That's a big feature. Let me first check the scale of remaining budget. Time is 2026-10-27 16:57; freeze line 2026-11-08. Plenty of days but each turn is costly. Let's be efficient but thorough.

Let me look at existing revision store functions and the revised resume read path, plus the shared index/exports conventions. Then write the plan (P11 C1) and implement.

Let me check revisedOf in repo.ts and how web renderRevision works.

</details>

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

I now understand the data model well. Let me design C1.

**C1 简历版本对比**：用户采纳改写后 → 用改完的简历作为输入，再跑一遍 → 对比两次的判定变化。

Design considerations:
1. 需要一个"从改完的简历派生一场新会话"的入口（clone session with revised resume text + 标记 parent session）。
2. 对比页：主张级判定变化（C1 solid→unsupported 之类），但注意主张文本可能变了（改写把句子改了），所以不能按 claim id 对齐 —— 需要按锚点原文 + 新文本对齐，或者干脆按类别/统计聚合对齐。
3. 这其实是 P12 大功能②成长档案 的基础。

Let me think about the simplest defensible design (and it must be genuinely useful, not gimmicky):

- schema: session 表加 `parent_session_id TEXT`（v10 迁移，加列）。表示"这一场是用上一场的改完简历重跑的"。
- 新建接口 `POST /api/sessions/:id/rerun`：拿 source 会话的 revised_resume.text 作为 resumeText，其余（JD、面试官阵容、追问强度和改写风格）继承，新建一场会话，meta 里记 `parent: <id>`。
- 对比读侧：`GET /api/sessions/:id/compare`（$id 是新会话），返回 diff 视图。
- 对齐策略：这是关键设计点。改写后的句子会变，所以 claim id 对不上。可用两种对齐：
  - 文本相似度对齐（claim text 的 token 相似度 + 类别）——但这很容易做成"看起来聪明其实不准"。
  - **按类别聚合 + 明确列出"上次说了但这次没出现的"与"这次新出现的"**。

我认为最诚实的设计是：**三条腿部**
  a. 总体：判定分布变化（solid/overstated/unsupported/contradicts/unanswerable 各几条 → 各几条），可用性问题数。
  b. 类别：每个类别（CATEGORY_ORDER 七类）的判定分布变化 → 回答"哪个类别总被打回"。
  c. 主张级对齐：用文本相似度做匹配，但**明确标注匹配置信度**，且只在相似度足够高时配对，其余归为「这次没再提」「这次新提的」。

其实先别过度设计。让我看看 shared 里有没有已有的文本相似度工具，以及 verdict 的类型。

另外考虑：C1 的价值主张 —— "改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变"。真实用户走完一场会拿到建议，采纳后简历变了，再跑一遍看是否还站得住。这是核心闭环，很值得做。

Let me check what's available in shared: shortfall.ts (categoryShortfalls), verdict types.

我先读一下 shared/domain.ts 和 shortfall.ts 看看结构。

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now let me plan C1. 

C1: 简历版本对比 — "改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变". The flow: user has a session with claims/verdicts and revised resume. Then they create a new session with the revised resume text. We need to link sessions as versions and compare verdicts.

Design decisions:
1. How to link? A "parent session id" — new session records `meta.derivedFrom = <parentId>` and `meta.versionOf = <rootId>`. Or explicitly: `POST /api/sessions` accepts `derivedFrom`. Better: a dedicated endpoint `POST /api/sessions/:id/rerun` that creates a new session using the revised resume text (if exists, else original), copying the panel/difficulty/style/jds from the parent, and records `meta.derivedFrom = parentId`, `meta.lineage = root`.

2. Comparison view: `GET /api/sessions/:id/compare?with=<otherId>` or a lineage endpoint `GET /api/sessions/:id/lineage` that returns all sessions in the chain with per-claim verdicts.

Key: claims get re-extracted with new ids C1..Cn. So comparing "claim by claim" across versions is not straightforward — the claim texts differ. Match by anchor overlap? By claim text similarity? This is the interesting design problem.

Approach: match claims across versions by **resume text span overlap** — since both versions derive from the same resume, the revised text's offsets differ. Hmm.

Better approach: match by **text similarity** on the claim text (normalized), falling back to category. Use a deterministic similarity: normalized claim text exact match first; else token/char-bigram Jaccard above threshold. Since revisions are small edits, most claims will be near-identical.

Actually more robust and simpler to explain: match by **anchor quote overlap** in the resume. But the resume changed...

Let me think about what the demo needs to show: "改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变" — e.g. C5 was unsupported → after rewrite it's solid. And which claims disappeared (因为改写后那句不再可被追问), which are new.

Design: `packages/shared/src/compare.ts` with a pure function `compareClaims(before: JudgedClaimLike[], after: JudgedClaimLike[]): ClaimDelta[]`, matching by normalized text similarity:
- normalize: strip whitespace/punctuation, lowercase
- similarity: char bigram Dice coefficient (or Jaccard) — deterministic, no dependency
- greedy match pairs with similarity >= MATCH_THRESHOLD (e.g. 0.6), highest similarity first; each side matched once
- outcome kinds: `improved` (WEAKNESS_RANK decreased), `regressed`, `same`, `resolved`(消失/不再出现 - in before only), `added` (in after only)

And a summary: `compareSummary` counting each kind, plus `verdictTimeline`.

That's a solid, explainable core. Plus persistence: link sessions.

DB: add `meta.derivedFrom` — meta is JSON, no schema change needed! Good, avoids v10. But wait, AGENTS/D44 rule says "字段只要在界面上出现过，就必须落到业务表". meta_json IS a business column, it's persistent and read back. Difficulty and style both live in meta. So derivedFrom in meta is consistent.

Hmm, but a lineage needs efficient queries — listSessions gives all sessions; filtering by meta.derivedFrom in JS is fine (max 50).

Let me design endpoints:
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/restart` (or `/derive`): body optional `{resumeText?}` — default: if the parent has a revised resume, use it; else 400 with a human message. Creates a new session copying jobTitle, jds, panel, difficulty, style, mode/model. Returns the new session's created payload + `derivedFrom`.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/lineage`: returns the chain from root → ... → this session, each with `{id, createdAt, resumeText? no—resumeHash, label(第 N 版), claimCount, verdictCounts, shortfall}`.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/compare/:otherId`? Simpler: `GET /api/sessions/:id/compare` → compares this session against its parent (if any) and returns deltas.

Let me define: `GET /api/sessions/:id/compare` returns `{before: {...}, after: {...}, deltas: ClaimDelta[], summary, notes}` where before is the parent version. If no parent → 200 with `parent: null` and empty deltas (界面显示「这是第一版」).

Frontend: a new pane/tab showing 「和上一版比」 list: 变好 X 条 / 变差 Y 条 / 依然站不住 Z 条 / 已消失 / 新出现. Plus a button 「改完再跑一遍」 in RevisionBar.

Let me look at the revisions.ts (web) client and RevisionBar to understand how revision view is surfaced, plus history panel component (HistoryPanel? maybe in App.tsx). Let me check components list — no HistoryPanel; history is probably in App.tsx or IntakePanel.

Now, C1 design: 简历版本对比 —— "改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变". Concretely: after accepting revisions, user can launch a new session with the revised resume text (same JD, same panel, same difficulty/style), then compare verdicts claim-by-claim across the two sessions. Since claim ids are renumbered per session (C1..Cn) and text differs, matching must be by anchor/text similarity.

Design decision: comparison needs claim pairing. Options:
(a) anchor-based: use quote text similarity — the revised text changed only at accepted spans, so most quote substrings still exist in the new resume. Match by quote occurrence (exact anchor resolve in other resume). For changed claims, the old quote no longer exists → those are exactly "the claims that changed".
(b) sequence alignment.

Best: implement in shared a pure function `compareSessions` that takes two claim lists (with verdicts) and pairs by quote: for each old claim, try to find its quote (or its revised text) in the new session's claims by normalized text match; unmatched old claims that were revised get paired with the new claim whose anchor overlaps the revised span region.

Simpler robust approach: pair by anchor coordinates on the ORIGINAL resume? But new session's anchor coordinates are on the revised resume.

Let me think about what's practical and demonstrable: The revised resume text is derived from original by replacements/insertions. We know applied revisions with original span + new text. If we run the new session on the revised text, its claims anchor into the revised text. To pair old claim → new claim: apply movedSpans (already in shared) to map old claim's span into the new text coordinates; then find the new-session claim whose span overlaps that mapped span, or whose quote equals the mapped substring.

That's principled and uses existing `movedSpans`. And it's a genuinely nice technical story: 版本对比靠的是「坐标搬运」，不是模型猜。

But wait — C1's flow: the new session uses revised text; claims are re-extracted by the model, so counts differ. Pairing by mapped span overlap ≥ some threshold.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now design C1.

C1: 简历版本对比（改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变）

Design思考：
- 用户在一场会话里改了简历（采纳改写），得到改完的简历全文。C1 要做的是：用这份改完的简历再开一场新会话（同 JD、同阵容、同档位、同风格），跑完之后把两场的判定逐条对比：哪条主张站得住了、哪条还是不行、新增了哪些主张、消失了哪些。
- 关键设计问题：怎么把「改了之后的简历」和「改之前的简历」对应起来？主张是模型抽的，两次抽取可能数量、顺序、措辞都不同。要用锚点/文本相似度做匹配。

匹配策略（这是 C1 的技术核心）：
1. 主张级匹配不是按 id（C1..Cn 是会话内编号，两场完全无关）。
2. 也不是简单的逐字比（改完的句子措辞已经变了）。
3. 合理的口径：**按锚点区间的原文片段做「归一化 + 相似度」匹配**，或者更稳的做法：在 applyRevisions 时保留「原文区间 → 新文本区间」的映射（movedSpans 已经做了），所以改完的简历里，被改过的那一段在新文本里的位置是已知的；然后第二场会话抽出的主张锚点如果落在这些新区间内，就认为它是「同一条主张的改写版」。

这是很漂亮的思路：版本对比不需要文本相似度算法，因为我们已经知道哪一段被改过。用 movedSpans 把「原文区间」映射到「新文本区间」，第二场的主张锚点落在哪个新区间里，就归到哪条老主张。剩下锚不到任何已知区间的，就是「新出现的主张」；老主张中没有被任何新主张覆盖的，就是「消失的主张」。

但要小心：改完之后句子变长/变短，模型重新抽主张时锚点可能超出原区间边界。所以匹配应该做成「重叠度」判断，而不是包含。

另外还有一层：如果用户改了简历但第二场沿用旧的 markdown 报告，判定标签怎么比？VerdictKind 五值：solid 站得住 / overstated 表述过度 / unsupported 证据缺失 / contradicts 与原文矛盾 / unanswerable 无法回答。对比要给出「变好 / 变坏 / 没变」。

再加上：怎么保证「改完再跑一遍」是可复现的？第二场会话必须记录 parentSessionId 与 baseRevision digest，这样报告里能说清「这一版是从哪一场改出来的、改了什么」。

工程上要做的：
1. schema v10：session 加 parent_session_id？或者放 meta。按 D44「字段只要在界面上出现过，就必须落到业务表」——但 parentSessionId 也是界面上会显示的（「这一版是从 2026-10-27 那场改出来的」）。嗯，但 meta 也是列（meta_json），已经落库。既有先例：difficulty / style 都放 meta。panel 也放 meta。所以放 meta 是一致做法。
   不过 C1 需要一个查询：「这个会话的父会话是谁」「这个父会话有哪些子会话」。按 meta 查需要全表扫。会话数量小（个人工具），可以先全表扫。但更干净的是加列。
   
   Hmm. 我倾向于加列 `parent_id TEXT` + 索引，因为这是关系，而且 version chain 需要按 parent 查子会话。SCHEMA_VERSION → 10，migrateV10 加列（ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN，SQLite 支持）。
   
   等等，但 migrated 顺序要求「不重建表」。ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN 是安全的。

2. shared 纯函数：packages/shared/src/compare.ts
   - `ClaimDiff` / `VersionDiff` 类型
   - `matchClaims(base: JudgedClaim[], next: JudgedClaim[], spans)` — 按「新主张锚点落在哪个被改过的新文本区间」归属
   - `verdictShift(from, to)` → 'better' | 'worse' | 'same'
   - `versionDiff(...)` 汇总：变好的、变坏的、没变的、新增的、消失的
   - `diffHeadline(...)` 一句话结论

3. 服务端：
   - `POST /api/sessions/:id/fork` — 用这份会话「改完之后」的简历再开一场，记住 parent
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/compare`（或无 parent 参数时自动找 parent）→ diff view

   替代方案：C1 的 fork 功能在 replay 模式下，如果没有卡带，就明确报错并提示「离线演示不覆盖这条路，请切到联网模式」。但这会削弱「无 Key 也能跑通全体」的卖点。

   我倾向于补齐卡带，因为演示视频里「改完再跑一遍看进步」是很强的一段。

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Let me now plan C1 design concretely and start implementing. First, I want to decide the data model for C1.

C1: 简历版本对比（改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变）

Design: A session that has a revised resume (revised_resume row) can be "forked" into a new session using the revised text as its resume. Then we compare two sessions' claim verdicts.

Simplest, most honest design:
- New table `session_origin` or just meta field `parentSessionId` on the new session. Actually meta already is a JSON blob; adding `parent: {sessionId, resumeDigest}` is cheap and needs no migration. But AGENTS.md rule D44 says "字段只要在界面上出现过，就必须落到业务表". Hmm — that rule was: Probe.requirementId optional in type but not stored in table. So a field shown in UI must be persisted in a business table. meta_json is persisted though... but the rule intent is queryability. For C1 we need to list "which sessions are versions of the same resume lineage" — a table with an index is better: `resume_lineage` table (session_id PK, parent_id, root_id, digest, created_at). That's schema v10.

Let me think about what C1's user story is:
1. User runs session A. Gets verdicts + suggestions.
2. Accepts revisions → revised resume text.
3. Clicks "改完再跑一遍" → creates session B seeded with the revised resume text, same JD, same panel/difficulty/style, and meta records parent = A.
4. After B runs, UI shows a comparison view: per claim category / per claim (matched by text or anchor?) how verdicts changed; and a "总账" — before: x overstated, after: y.

Matching claims across sessions is the hard part: claim ids are re-numbered per session (C1..Cn). Claim text may change slightly because the resume text changed. So match by: (1) anchor quote normalized equality, (2) fallback text similarity. Better: for revised claims, the suggestion records claimId + before + after. So we know: in session A, claim C3 was rewritten from `before` to `after`. In session B, the resume contains `after` instead of `before`. So we can match B's claim whose quote contains/equals `after` (normalized) → maps to A's C3. Unchanged claims match by quote equality.

So the comparison key is the **normalized quote** — for session A claims use their anchor quote; for session B claims use their anchor quote; and additionally map A's claim's `after` text (from revision) to the same bucket. That yields:
- matched: A claim quote ≈ B claim quote (unchanged) → same key
- rewritten: A claim replaced by `after`; B claim quote ≈ after → key = A's claim id
- new: B claim with no A counterpart (model extracted differently)
- gone: A claim with no B counterpart

Then per key compare verdictA vs verdictB: improved / worsened / unchanged / new / removed.

That's a genuinely nice, explainable design and reuses normalizeWithMap from anchor.ts.

Let me now check what exists in shared: normalizeWithMap is exported from anchor.ts (used in revise.ts). Good.

Now think about scale of work. This is a big feature: shared comparison module + tests, server endpoint to fork a session, lineage table, report section, web UI. Time is limited (functionality freeze 2026-11-08, today 2026-10-27). C1, C2, C3 are all in P11. Let me aim for C1 done solidly, then C2 (history dashboard) which is mostly read + UI, then C3 (single-file HTML packaging).

Let me check the web App.tsx size and RevisionBar to know where to wire "改完再跑一遍".

Actually, let me first write the shared comparison module since it's pure and testable.

Plan for C1:
1. shared/src/compare.ts: types + functions
   - `COMPARE_STATUS` = 'improved' | 'worsened' | 'same' | 'new' | 'dropped'
   - `VerdictDelta` per claim pair
   - `compareVersions(before: CompareClaim[], after: CompareClaim[]): VersionDiff`
   - CompareClaim = { id, text, quote, category, verdict: VerdictKind | null }
   - matching by normalized quote, with rewrite hints: `before`/`after` override texts.
   
   Actually simpler: build key = normalized quote; also pass rewrites: list of {claimId, before, after} from revisions. For the "before" side, claims are keyed by their quote. For the "after" side, claims keyed by their quote. A rewritten claim in A: its quote is `before`-ish. In B: quote is `after`-ish. So to link them: for each A claim that was rewritten, compute keyB = normalize(after); then when scanning B claims, if normalize(quote) matches keyB → link.
   
   Implementation: 
   ```
   const keyOf = (s) => normalize(s).norm.trim()  // plus collapse
   // map B claims by key
   // for A claims: candidate keys = [keyOf(quote), keyOf(after) if rewritten]
   ```
   Also handle text fallback: if quote match fails, try `text` match? Quotes are the anchor, more reliable. Let's do quote-primary, and a secondary pass matching by normalized `text` for remaining unmatched. Hmm, careful about complexity. Let me do quote-primary + text-secondary, documented as "两轮匹配".
   
   Sorting/ordering: output rows sorted by A ordinal then new ones.

2. Server:
   - schema v10: table `lineage` (session_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, parent_id TEXT, root_id TEXT, label TEXT, created_at TEXT) + index on root_id. Hmm, do we need label? "改完再跑一遍" produces a child. label optional.
   
   Actually maybe simpler: table `session_version` (session_id PK, parent_session_id, root_session_id, resume_digest, created_at). Let's name `resume_version`.
   
   - repo: saveVersionOf / getVersionOf / listVersionsOfRoot / listVersionChain
   - app.ts: POST /api/sessions/:id/fork  → creates new session with revised resume text (requires revisedOf not null, else 400), copies jds, panel, difficulty, style, mode/model. Returns created session id + version info.
   - GET /api/sessions/:id/versions → the chain (root + siblings) with summaries (claims count, verdict counts, digest, createdAt, parent)
   - GET /api/sessions/:id/compare?against=:otherId → diff.
   
   Hmm, better: GET /api/sessions/:id/compare/latest or /compare?base=<id>. Let's do `GET /api/sessions/:id/compare` → compares this session against its parent (if it has one) — that's the natural "改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变". And `?against=<sessionId>` for explicit.

3. Web: a "版本对比" panel/section shown in the run result area or a new tab in one of the four panes. And a button in RevisionBar "改完再跑一遍" that forks.

4. Report: section「版本对比」when the session has a parent.

Now let me design C1 properly.

C1: 简历版本对比（改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变）

Design: a session runs against resume v1. User accepts revisions → revised resume text. Then "再跑一遍" creates a NEW session with the revised resume text, linked to the parent session. Then compare verdicts per claim across the two rounds.

Key design questions:
1. How to link? `session.meta.parentId` + `session.meta.origin: 'revision'`. New session created by POST /api/sessions/:id/rerun or the front-end creates a new session with the revised text and parentId.
2. Claims re-extracted in the new session → claim ids C1..Cn differ. So comparison must be by text similarity/anchor, not id. Need a matching algorithm: match claims between two versions by normalized text similarity (or by anchor overlap). This is the meat: `compareVersions(before, after)` in shared.

Matching claims across versions: for each claim in the new version, find the best match in the old version by text similarity. Since revisions are known (applied revisions with before→after), we actually have a strong signal: the new resume text = old text with specific spans replaced. So we can map old claim anchors through the applied revisions to new positions! `movedSpans` already does this for revisions. Better: build an offset mapping from old text to new text given the applied revisions, then map each old claim's anchor to new position; a claim whose anchor was replaced keeps a mapped region; then match the new claim that overlaps it.

Hmm, but a cleaner and more defensible approach: match by anchor overlap after applying the same revision set to the old claim anchors. Claims that got rewritten... their anchor is the changed span so the new claim's anchor will land near the new text.

Simpler robust approach: two-tier matching:
- Tier 1 (exact anchor mapping): compute the old→new offset map from the applied revisions; for each old claim, map [start,end). If the old claim's span was not itself replaced, its mapped span in new text should contain an identical quote → find new claim whose anchor overlaps that span (IoU or containment).
- Tier 2 (text similarity): for the rest, fall back to normalized text similarity (token/char bigram Jaccard or Levenshtein ratio) with a threshold, greedy best-match one-to-one.

But do we need claim-level matching at all for a meaningful product? The user story: "改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变". What the user cares about: (a) this claim that was judged 表述过度 — after I fixed it, does it now stand? (b) new overall verdict distribution. So claim-level linkage is the core.

Since the revised resume is a *derived* document, and the new session re-extracts claims independently, the mapping is genuinely uncertain. I should design it honestly: a `linkedBy` field per pair: 'anchor' (spans map through the revisions) | 'text' (similarity) | 'none'.

Also aggregate: verdict transitions matrix (solid→solid, overstated→solid, ...) plus a headline "N 条主张变得更站得住，M 条仍被打回".

Let me think about what actually happens after 采纳改写 and re-running:
- The resume text is changed (a few sentences rewritten). Segment/extract produces claims on the NEW text with new ids C1..Cn and new anchors.
- The claim that was "表述过度" now has a rewritten text that is more defensible → verdict becomes solid.

To link A-claim ↔ B-claim, the strongest available signal: the revision's `after` text. If we run B on the revised resume, B's claim anchor quote should contain/overlap the revision's after text. That's a real, principled link: **改写落地的那段文字就是联系两版的证据**. That's a nice innovation point consistent with "evidence grounding".

But implementing that requires accurate positions: revision records have original coords, and movedSpans gives new-text coords. So we know exactly where each revision landed in the new text. Then B's claim anchors landing within [newStart, newEnd] (or overlapping) link back to A's claim via claimId... but revision record has claimId from session A. So the link is: A.claimId → revision → new span in B's resume → B claims overlapping that span.

That's implementable and deterministic. Untouched claims (not revised) — how to link? Their original text still exists verbatim in B's resume at a shifted position (since revisions changed lengths). We could compute the offset mapping: applyRevisions gives applied revisions with original coords and after lengths, so we can build a monotone coordinate map from A-text to B-text. Then a claim not overlapping any revision maps directly: A.start → new position, and B claim with the same anchor quote at that position matches.

That's actually a clean approach: build a coordinate map A→B (unchanged segments shifted by delta; revised segments map to after-span), then for each A claim, find the mapped span; look for B claim whose anchor quote equals A's quote (if unrevised) or whose span overlaps the after-span (if revised).

**C1 = 同一份简历的两次体检对照**: 用户在会话 A 采纳改写后，点「拿改完的简历再跑一遍」→ 新建会话 B（resumeText = A 的 revisedResume.text，meta 里记 `previousSessionId`、`revisionDigest`）。然后界面提供「版本对比」面板：比较 A 与 B 的

Alternative matching that is simple and robust: **按锚点在「改后」文本中的位置**做坐标映射。I need a mapping from A's text coords to B's text coords. Since B's resume = A's revised text, and revised text = applyRevisions(A.resumeText, candidates) with known applied revisions (original coords + after strings), I can produce an exact piecewise map. That's deterministic, cheap (pure function in shared), and testable. Then:
- for each A claim: mapped interval [s', e'] in B text.
- B claims whose anchor overlaps [s', e'] with the same category... or just any overlap → match.
- If no B claim overlaps → 「改完这句没被再抽出来」(dropped).

For revised claims, the mapped interval is the after-span, and B's claim anchor will land inside it.

This is a genuinely nice mechanism: 「坐标映射把两版缝在一起」. And it reuses applyRevisions data.

But careful: what if user starts B without any revisions (raw re-run)? Then A→B map is identity if B.resumeText === A.resumeText.

Also B may be created from any two sessions of the same user (历史里的两场). Simpler API: `GET /api/sessions/:id/compare/:otherId` → comparison view. Rules: allow any two sessions; the compare computes:
- if the two resumes are identical text → identity mapping;

- else → fall back to quoting-based matching (normalized quote equality) and report 「两版简历没有直接改写关系，只能按原句对齐」.

Design of coordinate mapping:
```
export interface CoordMap { segments: {aStart, aEnd, bStart, bEnd}[] }  // aStart..aEnd maps linearly to bStart..bEnd (same length)
```
Simplest: build list of (aStart, aEnd, bStart) for unchanged stretches; revised regions map to [bStart, bStart + after.length] with flag revised.

Build from applied revisions sorted ascending (applyRevisions returns applied sorted ascending). Walk:
```
let cursor = 0; // in A text
let delta = 0;  // b - a offset for unchanged
for each applied rev in ascending:
  if (rev.start > cursor) push unchanged {aStart: cursor, aEnd: rev.start, bStart: cursor + delta}
  push changed {aStart: rev.start, aEnd: rev.end, bStart: rev.start + delta, revised: true}
  delta += rev.after.length - (rev.end - rev.start)
  cursor = rev.end
if (cursor < aText.length) push unchanged {cursor, aText.length, cursor + delta}
```
Verify: b length = aText.length + sum(delta changes) = revised text length. Good — assert equality with revised text length as a safety check.

mapSpan(aStart, aEnd): find segments overlapping; produce b span from start of first overlapping to end of last overlapping. For an unrevised claim fully inside unchanged stretch → same text. For claim overlapping a revised region → span covers the new text.

Then match A claims to B claims: for each A claim, mapped span; find B claims whose anchor [start,end) overlaps mapped span. Pick best: max overlap, tie → same category, then smaller id.

Also need reverse direction: B claims not matched = 「新出现的」; A claims not matched = 「消失的」.

Also verdict delta: matched pairs with oldKind vs newKind; classify: improved (rank up), worsened, unchanged. Use WEAKNESS_RANK from shortfall? It ranks weakness (higher = weaker?). Let me check shortfall.ts lines 26-35. Need to read.

Now let me design C1 concretely.

C1: 简历版本对比（改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变）

Core value: after adopting revisions (改完的简历), create a new session with the revised resume and run again; then compare the two sessions claim by claim: what changed in verdicts. This proves the product closes the loop.

Design:

**shared/src/version.ts** (pure functions):
- `VersionPair`, `ClaimDelta`
- Matching two sessions' claims: how? Claims are extracted independently; ids C1..Cn differ. Match by anchor quote overlap? After revision, the text changed. Better matching strategy: match by normalized quote similarity + category.

Design decision: match claims by **quote matching after normalization**:
1. exact same quote (trimmed) → same claim
2. normalized equal → same
3. otherwise: by text similarity (longest common subsequence ratio?) — keep it deterministic and explainable.

Actually simpler and more defensible: match by **anchor-normalized quote containment / overlap**: if one quote contains the other (>= 6 chars) with ratio >= 0.6 → matched (改写型：原文片段被改写，新主张引用的是改写后的句子).

Let me think about the real semantics. When user adopts suggestions:
- claim C3 "把响应时间从 2.4s 优化到 0.8s" → rewritten to "把核心接口的 P99 响应时间从 2.4s 降到 0.8s（样本：线上 7 天）"? Hmm, actually rewrite must not add numbers.
- The new run extracts a claim from the new sentence. Its quote differs.

So matching is inherently fuzzy. I need a deterministic, explainable matcher:

`matchClaims(before: JudgedClaimLike[], after: JudgedClaimLike[], opts)` → pairs + unmatched lists.

Matching tiers (each tier deterministic, greedy by sorted order):
1. **同锚**：normalized quote 相等 → confidence 'same'
2. **改写**：one normalized quote contains the other, or a token-overlap similarity ≥ threshold → 'rewritten'
3. 其他 → unmatched ('gone' / 'new')

Similarity: use character bigram Dice coefficient — deterministic, no dependency. Document threshold e.g. 0.55.

Then `claimDeltas` computed: for each pair, verdictBefore, verdictAfter, direction: improved / worsened / same / judged-difference.

Aggregates: `versionPlan` = counts of improved/worsened/same, headline text, and per-category.

This gives a crisp feature: 「改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变」+ 每个变化标出原因（引用原文对照）。

Also important: the comparison must be **honest about what cannot be compared**: claims added/removed. That's `appeared` / `disappeared` lists.

**server**: 
- schema v10: table `session_link` (session_id PRIMARY KEY, parent_session_id, created_at) — records "这一场是从哪一场改完再跑的". That's the field that must be persisted (rule D44).
- POST /api/sessions/:id/rerun → creates a new session with revised text (or original if none) + same panel/difficulty/style/jds, links parent. Returns new session id.
- GET /api/sessions/:id/compare?with=<parentId> or GET /api/sessions/:id/compare → auto-find parent via session_link, compute versionPlan.

Now let me look at how the pipeline builds SessionView + how listSessions works, to design C1.

Let me plan C1 design concretely.

C1 goal: 简历版本对比（改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变）.

Design: 
- Server: new endpoint to create a "对比" (comparison) between two sessions? Or auto-derive: when creating a new session, its resumeText may be based on a previous session's revised resume. So a link exists.
- Implementation plan: add `meta.derivedFrom` = source session id when creating a session from an existing session's revised resume ("改完再跑一遍" button). Then a comparison endpoint `GET /api/sessions/:id/compare` (or `/:id/compare/:otherId`) that diffs claim verdicts between the two sessions.

Comparison data: category shortfalls, verdict counts, per-claim... but claim ids are re-numbered per session and claim extraction may differ. So compare at the level of: verdict distribution, category shortfalls, and matched claims by anchor text overlap? Since resume text changed, claim anchors shift.

Better: compare by claim text similarity? Simpler and defensible: compare 三个层次：
1. 整体判定分布（solid/overstated/...）
2. 类别短板（categoryShortfalls）
3. 文本层面：改完的简历里，上一场被判「站不住」的那些句子，这一场还判不判站不住 —— 需要文本匹配。

Actually there's a clean deterministic approach: for each claim in the old session that had a verdict != solid, look up whether the new session has a claim whose anchor quote covers/overlaps the same region. But text changed.

Alternative grounded approach: use the revision records. The old session has revisions (before→after). The new session runs on the new text. We can map: old claim's anchor quote `before` — in the new text, the corresponding region is `after` (at movedSpans). So for each accepted revision, we know the new-text span. Then find a claim in the new session whose anchor intersects that span → that's "the same claim, re-judged". That's deterministic and evidence-grounded. 

That's a genuinely nice design: 用采纳记录做「同一个说法在改后文本里落在哪」的桥。For claims without revisions, we can also match by exact quote when the text around was untouched: locateQuote(newText, oldQuote) — if the old claim's quote still appears verbatim in the new resume, it's the same claim.

So matching algorithm (pure function in shared):
```
matchClaims(prev: {id, quote, start, end, verdict}[], revised: {text, applied: Revision[]}, next: {id, quote, start, end, verdict}[], nextText)
```
Steps:
1. For each prev claim, compute its "expected new span": apply the mapped shift. Use movedSpans(applied) to shift positions? movedSpans maps applied revisions to new-text spans. We can compute a position mapping function from old offsets to new offsets given the applied revisions (deterministic).
2. In the new session's claims, find the one whose anchor overlaps the expected span; if none, fall back to quote containment (locateQuote in newText, then overlap).

Simpler and more robust: 
- For each prev claim: compute expected span in new text via `shiftSpan(oldSpan, applied)`.
- Candidate next claims: those whose anchor range overlaps expected span by >= some ratio, OR whose quote is contained in expected new segment.
- If expected segment is the revision's after text, then next claim's quote likely is within it.

Hmm, but claim extraction from the new resume may produce a claim whose quote covers a larger/different region.

I think a solid, explainable approach:
- Build a mapping old→new offsets (`shiftPoint`) using applied revisions.
- For each prev claim with a verdict, expected new range = [shiftPoint(start), shiftPoint(end)] (for insertions before it, shift by inserted text length).
- Then for each next claim, compute overlap with expected range. Pick the best-overlapping next claim (max overlap / min length). If overlap ratio >= 0.5 with either side, treat as paired.
- Unpaired prev claims = 没再被问到的说法（可能因为原文已改）; unpaired next claims = 新出现的说法.

But we need next claims' verdicts; new session must be run to have verdicts.

Then the comparison view: 
- pairs: [{prevId, nextId, prevVerdict, nextVerdict, change: improved|worsened|same|new|gone}]
- counts of each change
- headline: 「比上一版多了 3 条站得住，C2 那条从表述过度变成站得住」

This is a meaty, valuable feature and uses existing pure functions.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I have a good picture. Let me plan C1 「简历版本对比（改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变）」.

Design思考:
- C1 的目标：用户采纳改写后，简历文本变了（revised_resume），可以「再跑一遍」并自动对比两场的判定。
- 关键设计问题：新一场会话的简历是新版本，主张锚点在**新文本**坐标上。要对比就必须建立两场主张之间的对应关系。

匹配策略（核心难点，也是技术文档的亮点）：不能靠 claimId（C1..Cn 每场重新编号，顺序可能变）。候选匹配方式：
1. 用 `before` 改写记录反推：新版简历是老版改出来的，我们可以保存「改动映射」，从而知道老版每个区间在新版中的位置。
2. 用文本相似度匹配主张（Levenshtein / 关键词）。

我认为最诚实、最可解释的是：**用改动记录建立坐标映射**（这是确定性的，不是猜的）。具体：`movedSpans(applied)` 给出每条改动在新文本中的区间；老版中未被改动的区间，其在新版中的偏移可由 delta 累积算出。所以老版老主张的锚点区间 [start,end) 映射到新版区间：
- 若该区间完全落在某条改动的原文区间内 → 被改动了，映射为「该改动在新版中的区间」（内容变了）
- 否则落在两个改动之间的空隙里 → 位置平移 delta

这样就能得到每条老主张在新版里的对应片段，然后在**新一场**的主张列表里找「锚点区间落在该映射区间内且重叠最大」的那一条 → 配对成功。配对方法不依赖模型，纯几何。

这个设计很棒，但代价：新版简历必须是从旧版改出来的（存在 revised_resume 且 digest 匹配）。如果用户拿了个完全不同的简历，就退回到「按文本相似度匹配」。

我倾向做混合：
- 优先「坐标映射」（可解释：这两条是同一条，因为我们知道你改了哪里）
- 退化时用「文本相似度」（标注为「按原文相似度配对」而不是「确定同一条」）

时间考虑：这是 P11 的 C1，预估 14–18 h 是 C1+C2+C3 三项一起。C1 大概 4-6h 工作量在 AI 手里可能 1-2h。

再看 C2 历史仪表盘、C3 单文件 HTML。用户还没明确让我做 C1/C2/C3，但上一轮的 Open objectives 说「按 m07544+m07596 执行第二阶段 → C1 → C2 → C3 → P12 → P13 → P14」。所以是的，继续做 C1。

不过：我应该先给用户一个简短的进度汇报吗？这是个长跑任务，用户说「你随时可以接管电脑」，要我全力冲。前面的模式是：AI 连续做，定期汇报。当前这一轮我应该继续干活。让我先做 C1。

现在让我设计 C1 的最小可用落点：

**数据面**：
- 不需要新表？对比结果是「派生」的：给定 sessionA（父）和 sessionB（子），可以现算。但需要记录「B 是从 A 的哪个版本跑出来的」。
- 我倾向：在 session.meta 里加 `origin: { sessionId, revisedDigest }` —— 建立会话时就带上「我是从这一场改完之后再跑的」。不进新表（meta 已经存在，且这是派生信息）。
- schema 升级？如果只用 meta，不需要 v10。但要谨慎：D44 的规则是「字段只要在界面上出现过，就必须落到业务表」。对比结果会在界面上出现…… 但对比结果是派生计算，不是输入。类比 `shortfallPlan`、`contractViews`——它们也是现算的，没有表。所以留 meta + 现算是合理的。

**shared 层**（纯函数，可单测，可回放）：
- `packages/shared/src/compare.ts`：
  - `mapOldSpan(oldStart, oldEnd, applied: Revision[], oldTextLength): {start,end} | null` — 坐标映射
  - `claimPairs(oldClaims, newClaims, applied, oldText): ClaimPair[]` — 配对
  - `comparePlans(oldPlan, newPlan): CompareSummary` — 判定怎么变
  - `VERDICT_TREND_LABEL`: improved / unchanged / regressed / appeared / disappeared

判定变化的方向语义：站得住是好的。WEAKNESS_RANK 已有：solid 0 ... unanswerable 4。差值 <0 = 变好了（weakness 降），>0 = 变差了，=0 = 没变。

- `CompareRow`：{ oldClaimId, newClaimId, quote(原文), oldVerdict, newVerdict, trend }
- `CompareSummary`：{ improved, unchanged, regressed, appeared, disappeared, headline }

**server 层**：
- `packages/server/src/compare.ts`：`compareWithOrigin(db, sessionId)` —— 读 session.meta.origin，取父会话，做配对，返回视图。
- HTTP：`GET /api/sessions/:id/compare` → { available: false, reason } 或 { available: true, ... }
- 建会话：POST /api/sessions 接受 `origin?: { sessionId, revisedDigest }`，校验父会话存在且 revised_resume.digest 与当前简历内容一致（防止「你以为在比同一版，其实不是」）。这条校验很重要：如果用户采纳后又手改了简历，digest 对不上，就明确拒绝并说明。
  实际上更好的做法：如果 resumeText 与父会话的 revised_resume.text 不相等 → 拒绝 origin（返回 400 或降级为无 origin）。我倾向：即使不等也允许跑，但明确告知「这一版不是你改出来的那一版」——不，容易让人困惑。严格拒绝更诚实：`originDigest` 必须匹配。

- 报告：新增一节「改完之后，判定怎么变」。这是 C1 的落点。

**web 层**：
- 采纳改写后，RevisionBar 里给一个按钮「改完再跑一遍」（data-shot=rerun），带着 origin 建新会话并跑。
- ClaimBoard 或新增一个 CompareBoard 展示趋势。

先看看 RevisionBar 和 App.tsx 的结构再定。让我先看 web 的关键文件大小。

另外考虑：C1 的「再跑一遍」在 replay 模式下怎么办？新会话的简历文本变了 → 提示词变了 → 卡带指纹变了 → 找不到卡带！这是个大问题。

离线演示：demo 场次没有采纳改写，所以 demo 不会走 C1。但 e2e 会走。e2e 用 replay 模式，采纳改写后重跑 → 卡带缺失 → 报错。

解决：e2e 里 C1 的测试用 record/scripted 模式？或者给 scripted-demo 也支持「改后简历」的卡带 —— 太复杂。

更实际：C1 重跑要求 live 或 record 模式（replay 下无法为任意改后简历预录卡带）。在界面上：如果是 replay 模式，「改完再跑一遍」按钮明确提示「离线回放没法为你改过的简历预先录卡带；这一遍需要联网或录制」（并禁用/引导切换）。

这是个真实的、必须讲清楚的产品限制，正好是技术文档的好素材。

那 e2e 怎么测 C1？可以：
- e2e 用 replay 测「按钮存在 + 提示文案正确 + 点击后得到明确错误提示」——这是诚实的行为测试。
- server 单测用 scripted 客户端测完整对比逻辑（用测试桩，不依赖卡带）。

好，这个方案可行。

关键算法设计（要写得很严谨，因为这是纯函数、要单测）：

坐标映射 mapOldSpanToNew(oldStart, oldEnd, applied, oldTextLen):
applied 是原文坐标、升序。对老主张区间 [s,e)：
- 遍历 applied，维护 delta。
- 找出与 [s,e) 重叠的改动。
- 如果没有重叠改动：newStart = s + delta（delta 为所有 start <= s 的改动造成的位移），newEnd = e + delta。
- 如果有重叠：这说明这条主张的原文被改过了。映射到「改动后那片区域」：newStart = 覆盖它的第一条改动的 start + deltaAtThatPoint，newEnd = 最后一条相关改动的 end + deltaAfter。内容已经变了，但区间有意义（用来在新版里找对应主张）。

配对 claimPairs(oldClaims, newClaims, mapping)：
- 对每条老主张，算映射区间 [ns, ne)。
- 在新主张里找 anchor.start 落在这个区间内（或与区间重叠最大）的那条。
- 一对一：一条新主张只能配一条老主张（先到先得，按重叠长度降序分配）。
- 没配上的老主张 → disappeared；没被配上的新主张 → appeared。

这个「重叠最大 + 一对一」是确定性贪心，可单测。

然后 comparePlans：
- 对每一对：trend = improved (rank 降) / unchanged / regressed (rank 升)
- 还有些边界：老主张有判定、新主张还没判（null）→ trend = 'pending'？

我把 trend 定义为：'improved' | 'unchanged' | 'regressed' | 'appeared' | 'disappeared' | 'pending'。pending 是「新版这条还没判出来」——跑一半的情况。

headline：类似 shortfallHeadline 的一句话结论。

映射函数：

```ts
export interface SpanMap {
  start: number;
  end: number;
  /** 这段原文被改写建议动过：内容已经不是原来那句 */
  changed: boolean;
}

export function mapOldSpan(start: number, end: number, applied: readonly Revision[]): SpanMap | null
```

applied 是原文坐标升序。逻辑：

```ts
let delta = 0;
let firstHit: { start: number; shift: number } | null = null;
let lastEnd = -1;
let lastShift = 0;
for (const item of applied) {
  const itemStart = item.start + delta;  // 这条改写在**新文本**里的起点
  const overlaps = item.start < end && item.end > start;  // 与老区间重叠
  // 插入型 (start===end) 的重叠判定：item.start > start && item.start < end
  ...
}
```

插入型（start===end）怎么算重叠？插入点在 [start, end] 内算重叠。用 `item.start <= end && item.start >= start`。

简化：把每条 applied 视为「原文区间 [item.start, item.end)，插入型退化为点」。重叠判定：
- 非插入型：item.start < end && item.end > start
- 插入型：item.start >= start && item.start <= end

如果有重叠 → changed = true。

新坐标计算：
- 对未被改动的区间：newStart = start + 累计位移（所有 end <= start 的改动），newEnd = end + 同一累计位移。
- 对改动的：newStart = start + 累计位移（改动之前的），newEnd = ... 复杂。

我简化：只要 changed 就把整个映射区间扩展到覆盖所有相关改动的新区间：

```ts
// 走向：先定位第一个上界
```

实现思路（清晰版）：

```ts
export function mapOldSpan(start: number, end: number, applied: readonly Revision[]): SpanMap | null {
  let delta = 0;          // 已经扫过的改动造成的累计位移
  let newStart: number | null = null;
  let newEnd: number | null = null;
  let changed = false;

  for (const item of applied) {
    const insertion = item.start === item.end;
    const overlaps = insertion
      ? item.start >= start && item.start <= end
      : item.start < end && item.end > start;

    // 这条改动在**新文本**里占据的区间
    const spanStart = item.start + delta;
    const spanEnd = spanStart + item.after.length;

    if (overlaps) {
      changed = true;
      if (newStart === null) newStart = spanStart;
      newEnd = spanEnd;
    }

    delta += item.after.length - (item.end - item.start);
  }

  if (!changed) {
    // 没被改过：整体平移。delta 此刻是全部改动的总位移，但只有**位于区间之前**的才算。
    // 重新扫一遍更直白：
  }
}
```

啊，不对。未改动时我不能用最终 delta。需要只在 `item.end <= start` 时才计入。

重写：

```ts
export function mapOldSpan(start, end, applied): SpanMap | null {
  let delta = 0;
  let newStart = -1;
  let newEnd = -1;
  let changed = false;

  for (const item of applied) {
    const insertion = item.start === item.end;
    const spanStart = item.start + delta;
    const spanEnd = spanStart + item.after.length;
    const overlaps = insertion
      ? item.start > start && item.start < end   // 严格落在内部才算动到这条主张的原文
      : item.start < end && item.end > start;

    if (overlaps) {
      changed = true;
      if (newStart < 0) newStart = Math.min(spanStart, start + delta);
      newEnd = Math.max(spanEnd, ...);
    } else if (item.end <= start) {
      // 完全在这条主张之前：它的位移要算进去
      // 不需要特殊处理，delta 已经累积
    }
    delta += item.after.length - (item.end - item.start);
  }
  ...
}
```

这变复杂了。用更简单的两遍法：

```ts
function shiftBefore(pos: number, applied: readonly Revision[]): number {
  let delta = 0;
  for (const item of applied) {
    if (item.end <= pos) delta += item.after.length - (item.end - item.start);
    else break;   // applied 按 start 升序，end 不一定升序，但正常改写不重叠
  }
  return delta;
}
```

applied 保证不重叠（applyRevisions 保证），所以 end 单调不减，break 是安全的。但插入型（start===end）会插在同一位置，需要 item.end <= pos 处理 —— 插入型 end===start，如果 start <= pos 就计数。对插入型用 `item.start <= pos`？用 `item.end <= pos` 当 start===end 时 = start <= pos，OK。

不过 break 有风险。改用完整遍历不 break，安全些（applied 通常 <20 条）：

```ts
function shiftBefore(pos: number, applied: readonly Revision[]): number {
  let delta = 0;
  for (const item of applied) {
    if (item.end <= pos) delta += item.after.length - (item.end - item.start);
  }
  return delta;
}
```

然后：

```ts
export function mapOldSpan(start, end, applied): SpanMap {
  const hits = applied.filter((item) => overlaps(item, start, end));
  if (hits.length === 0) {
    const shift = shiftBefore(start, applied);
    return { start: start + shift, end: end + shift, changed: false };
  }
  const first = hits[0];
  const last = hits[hits.length - 1];
  const newStart = Math.min(start + shiftBefore(start, applied), first.start + shiftBefore(first.start, applied));
  const lastStart = last.start + shiftBefore(last.start, applied);
  const newEnd = Math.max(end + shiftBefore(end, applied), lastStart + last.after.length);
  return { start: newStart, end: newEnd, changed: true };
}
```

嗯，shiftBefore(end) 对插入在 end 处的会算进去。可以接受。

这个够用了。清晰、可测。

配对：

```ts
export interface ClaimPair {
  /** 老那一场的主张 id；新版才有的那条是 null */
  oldId: string | null;
  newId: string | null;
  /** 展示用：老版原文那句（新版找不到对应时也要能讲清楚是哪一条） */
  before: string;
  /** 新版对应的那句；新版没有就是 null */
  after: string | null;
  oldVerdict: VerdictKind | null;
  newVerdict: VerdictKind | null;
  trend: CompareTrend;
  /** 配对依据：map = 坐标映射（这几条确定是同一条），text = 文本相同兜底 */
  via: 'anchor' | 'text' | 'none';
}
```

配对算法：
1. 对每条老主张算映射区间。
2. 对每条新主张，看它的 anchor.start 是否落在某条老主张的映射区间 [m.start, m.end) 内；且要求重叠长度最大。实际上新主张的 anchor 应该完整落在映射区间内。
3. 一对一贪心：先建候选 (oldIndex, newIndex, score=重叠长度)，按 score 降序、oldIndex 升序分配。

兜底：如果坐标映射没配上（例如用户手改了简历），可以按文本完全相同配对。但我在服务端会强制校验 digest（简历必须是父会话改出来的那一版），所以映射总是可靠的。不过为了健壮（也为了纯函数能独立测试），加文本兜底。

hmm，实际上如果 digest 强制相等，文本兜底就没用了。但如果用户「改了简历又改了回来」…… 算了，加兜底成本低，就加。

对了，还要考虑：`pending` 档——新版还没判（null）。如果 newVerdict 为 null 而 oldVerdict 有值 → trend = 'pending'。

如果两者都 null → 'pending' 也算。

trend 计算：
```ts
function trendOf(oldV: VerdictKind | null, newV: VerdictKind | null): CompareTrend {
  if (newV === null) return 'pending';
  if (oldV === null) return 'pending';  // 老版没判，没法比
  const gap = WEAKNESS_RANK[newV] - WEAKNESS_RANK[oldV];
  if (gap < 0) return 'improved';
  if (gap > 0) return 'regressed';
  return 'unchanged';
}
```

CompareReport:
```ts
export interface CompareReport {
  pairs: ClaimPair[];
  improved: number;
  unchanged: number;
  regressed: number;
  pending: number;
  appeared: ClaimPair[];
  disappeared: ClaimPair[];
  headline: string;
}
```

headline 例如：「12 条主张里 7 条变好了、2 条变差、3 条没变；新版另多出 1 条。」

Now let me plan C1. C1: resume version comparison — after revisions accepted, re-run the interview and compare verdicts. Design:

New shared module `versions.ts` (or `compare.ts`) that computes a diff between two sessions of the same resume lineage:
- Claim-level comparison: match claims by text between v1 and v2 (claims are re-extracted, ids C1..Cn differ), so match by anchor quote normalized text or by claim text similarity.
- Verdict transitions: solid→overstated, etc.
- Category-level shortfall changes.

But "改完再跑一遍" requires running a new session with the revised resume. So we need a link: session -> parentSessionId, resume lineage. Store: session.meta already JSON. But F54 rule: "字段只要在界面上出现过，就必须落到业务表". Hmm, difficulty and style are in meta, so meta is acceptable as an established pattern. But maybe better to add a real column? Let's keep consistency: difficulty/style live in meta. Adding `parent_id` to session table would be schema v10. Hmm.

Let me think about how much work this is and be efficient. Design for C1:

1. shared/src/versions.ts — pure functions:
   - `alignClaims(prev: ClaimLike[], next: ClaimLike[]): AlignedClaim[]` — match by normalized quote, then by normalized text overlap.
   - `verdictTransitions(...)` — counts.
   - `compareVersions(prev, next): VersionComparison` with per-claim rows: {status: 'improved'|'regressed'|'same'|'new'|'gone', prevVerdict, nextVerdict}
   - `versionHeadline(...)`
   - `WEAKNESS_RANK` reuse from shortfall.

2. server: 
   - store: add `parent_id` column to session (schema v10) — or store in meta? I think a real column is cleaner and matches D44's spirit. Actually session table already has meta_json; difficulty/style are there. Adding parent_id as a column means schema v10 + migration. That's a real, defensible change: "版本链".
   - API: `GET /api/sessions/:id/versions` → returns lineage chain with comparison; `POST /api/sessions` accepts `parentId`.
   - Front end: RevisionBar already? Add a "改完再跑一遍" button that creates a new session with the revised resume text + same jd/panel/difficulty/style, parentId = current. Then after run, show comparison panel.

3. Web: new component `VersionPanel.tsx` or extend history. Plus report sections.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now let me think about what C1 (简历版本对比 - resume version comparison) actually means. Let me plan the design.

C1: 简历版本对比（改完再跑一遍）— after accepting revisions, run again and compare how verdicts changed. This is "same resume, improved, re-run the interview, compare verdicts per claim".

Key design question: how do we link claim across versions? Claims are extracted anew each session, ids are C1..Cn re-numbered each session. So we need an anchor-based or text-based matching.

The core idea: 版本对比 = 两次会话（同一份 JD 或没有 JD）之间，找出「上一版有问题的那些主张，这一版变成了什么」。

Matching strategy: since the revised resume contains the modified sentences, claim anchors differ. Options:
1. Match by category + fuzzy text similarity.
2. Match by the revision chain: the second session's resume text = first session's applied text. So a claim in session 2 whose anchor overlaps a revision's new span can be traced back to the claim that produced the revision... but only if we know the lineage.

Actually simpler & more robust and honest: we can build a **key-based** match: normalize the claim anchor quote (or claim text) and match across sessions. But after rewriting, the text differs.

Better: use the revision record. Session 1's accepted revisions map claimId → (start,end,before,after) in the resume. Session 2's resume text = session1's revised text. So for each claim in session 2, check whether its anchor falls inside a region that came from a revision. We have `movedSpans` in revise.ts which gives new coordinates in the applied text. So we can build a mapping: for each revision, its new span in applied text → the source claimId of session 1.

But the claim in session 2 might anchor to a broader region overlapping the new span. We can use interval overlap.

Hmm, but that only works for claims that survived. Claims that disappeared (the problematic sentence was deleted) won't have a counterpart — and that's an important signal too: 「上一版这条欠证据的主张，这一版被改掉了/删掉了」.

Let me think about a defensible, simple, deterministic matching approach that's pure-function testable in shared:

**Approach: 三路匹配（anchor 重叠 → 文本相似 → 类别兜底）**

Actually, let me think about what's the most honest and simplest. Two sessions:
- base session (上一版)
- next session (这一版)

For each claim in base with a non-solid verdict, we want to know what happened in next.

Matching signal: normalized text similarity between base claim's anchor quote and next claim's anchor quote. Since the rewrite changes wording, similarity may drop. But we also have the revision's `after` text — the rewritten sentence. So the next session's claim about that sentence will anchor onto the `after` text, which we know the source claimId for.

Let me design a clean approach:

`compareVersions(base: VersionSnapshot, next: VersionSnapshot): VersionDiff`

Where VersionSnapshot = { sessionId, resumeText, claims: JudgedClaimLike[] with anchor {start,end,quote}, revised... }

Hmm. This is getting complex. Let me consider what the user-facing value is: 「改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变」. The UI: pick a previous session (same resume lineage) and see side-by-side: 上一版 C2 表述过度 → 这一版 站得住. Plus overall: 站得住从 4 条变 7 条.

So the diff needs:
1. **匹配**（哪条对哪条）
2. **变化**（improved / worsened / unchanged / 消失了 / 新出现）
3. **总体**（solid 数、欠证据数、按类别）

And it must be honest about unmatched ones: 「这一版的措辞变化太大，匹配不上」should be surfaced not hidden.

Matching algorithm design (pure, deterministic):

For each base claim, score each next claim:
- anchor overlap: if base anchor and next anchor are in "corresponding" regions... but coordinates are in different texts. Cannot use raw overlap.
- So text-based: normalized quote similarity.

Use a simple deterministic similarity: longest common subsequence length ratio, or bigram Dice coefficient. Dice coefficient on character bigrams is simple and deterministic. threshold e.g. 0.5.

Additionally the revision lineage: if session2 was created from session1's applied text (we can detect: session2.resumeText === session1.appliedText), then we know the mapping from revision new-spans to base claimIds. A next claim whose anchor overlaps a revision new-span inherits that base claimId — this is a strong signal (score 1.0).

That lineage detection is nice: it makes the "改完再跑" flow first-class. Let me check if the app tracks this. There's `revised_resume` table with text/digest. And sessions have resumeHash.

Is there a "从这一版再跑一遍" button? Probably not yet. Let me check the web app for where revisions/sessions are created.

Let me look at the existing UI and http endpoints to see extension points. Let me read App.tsx (probably large) and api.ts.

Given the effort budget, let me be efficient: read api.ts fully, and grep App.tsx for handleAccept etc.

Actually, let me first decide scope. C1 deliverables:
1. shared/version.ts — pure diff logic + tests
2. server: endpoint GET /api/sessions/:id/compare?to=<id> or auto-find previous version; plus "再跑一遍" from revised resume (POST /api/sessions/:id/rerun)
3. web: a compare panel
4. report: a section
5. e2e + screenshots + docs + commits

That's a lot but it's the pattern I've been following. Let me start by reading the relevant code.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Let me look at the rest of api.ts and the App.tsx to see where to insert C1 UI. But first: think about what C1 actually is.

C1: 简历版本对比（改完再跑一遍）— Compare resume versions: after applying revisions, run again and see how verdicts change.

Design: a "版本对比" view. Needs:
1. Server: an endpoint that compares two sessions (the original session and a re-run session on the revised resume), aligning claims by... what? Claims are renumbered C1..Cn each session, so aligning by id is wrong. Need alignment by text/anchor.

Key design problem: how to align claims across two runs? Since the revised resume has different text, anchors shift. Options:
- Match by claim text similarity (normalized), or
- Match by the revision linkage: the second session's claim whose anchor overlaps a revision span.

Actually better: since we know which revisions were accepted (claimIds), the re-run session's claims can be matched to original claims by textual overlap of the anchor quote. Let's think about the "honest" approach: match by normalized claim text (the claim text is a paraphrase-ish but usually close to the quote). Hmm.

Alternative simpler and more defensible: match by anchor quote after normalization — the second run's claim anchor quote appears in the revised text; the original claim's quote is what it replaced. If a claim was rewritten, the quote changed.

Hmm, what's the cleanest? Let me think about what makes a good product story and is technically honest.

Design proposal for C1:
- A "版本" concept: session A (v1, original resume). User accepts revisions → revised text. User clicks 「用改后的简历再跑一遍」→ creates session B with resumeText = revised text, and records in session B meta: `{derivedFrom: A_id, version: 2, basis: 'revised'}`.
- Then a comparison endpoint `GET /api/sessions/:id/compare` (or `/api/versions?root=`) that, given the root session, walks the version chain and builds a per-claim alignment.

Alignment: let me define `alignVersions(oldClaims, newClaims)`. Match rules in order:
1. Exact normalized text equality of claim.text.
2. Otherwise, by anchor quote normalized equality.
3. Otherwise, token overlap (Jaccard over bigrams of the normalized text) above a threshold (e.g. 0.5), greedy best match.

And record match quality (exact / quote / fuzzy) so the UI can say "推理对齐" honestly. Mismatched ones show as 「这一版新增」/「这一版没有了」.

That's a real algorithm with a defensible design, testable as pure functions in shared. 

Hmm, but is there a simpler alignment signal? We DO have the revision record: accepted revisions map claimId → before/after. So for old claim C3 with revision before="主导了" after="参与了", the new resume contains "参与了...". The new claim's anchor quote would presumably be the new text. So we could match by the revision's `after` text. That's a third signal.

I think a layered approach with explicit confidence labeling is good and honest. Let me keep it to: exact text → anchor quote → revision after (for rewritten claims) → bigram similarity ≥ threshold. Report `matchKind`.

Now what does the comparison show? The core value: **判定怎么变**. Table:
| 主张 (v1) | v1 判定 | v2 判定 | 变化 |
And a summary headline: "8 条里 3 条从「证据缺失」变成「站得住」，1 条变差，2 条这版没有对应"。

Plus: 类别层面的变化 (reuse categoryShortfalls on both and diff).

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now let me design C1: 简历版本对比（改完再跑一遍）.

Let me look at what's needed. The core: a session's revised resume (applied) can be used as input for a new session, and then compare the two sessions' verdicts claim-by-claim, showing improvement/regression.

Let me check existing structures: listSessions, the session meta, and how a new session is created from resumeText. Let me read app.ts session creation handler and look at how the web decides parent-child.

Let me plan concretely:

C1 功能设计:
1. "基于这版改写再跑一遍" — 从某个已有会话的采纳后简历（applied text）新建一场会话，meta 记 `fromSession: <id>`、`version: n`。
2. 对比视图：两场会话的判定按主张对齐。对齐策略：因为 id 每场都从 C1 重编，不能按 id 对齐；必须按**文本/锚点**对齐。改后简历的文本变了，怎么办？

关键设计问题：如何对齐「同一句主张在两版之间」。方案：
- 用 originalClaim 的锚点 quote 归一化后的文本做 key？改后简历的那条主张会锚到改写后的文本（after），文本不同。
- 更稳的方案：用 `revise.ts` 的 movedSpans / 或者「改写来源」——如果第二场的简历是由第一场的 accepted revisions 生成的，我们可以在建第二场时记录 `lineage: {fromSessionId, acceptedClaimIds}`。
- 对齐规则：改后的文本 = 原文区间被 after 替换。第二场抽出来的主张如果锚点落在改写后的文本里，可以反查它对应第一场的哪条主张：通过「在改写后文本里的区间 → 映射回原文区间」的逆映射。这正是 movedSpans 干的事（新文本坐标）。

实际上有更简单且可解释的方案：**按「原文区间」对齐**。第二场会话的简历是 revised resume。若要映射回第一场的原文坐标，需要 revision 的 spans（movedSpans 给的是新文本坐标）. 我们可以在建会话时，把第一场的 accepted revisions 作为「血缘」记在第二场 meta 里，然后：
- 第二场的一条主张，其 anchor 区间 A' 在改写后文本中。
- 若 A' 落在某个改写后的 span 内且该 span 属于主张 Cn → 对齐到 Cn。
- 否则（落在未改动区域）→ 用 revised→original 的偏移映射（applyRevisions 已有 spans 信息可逆算）找到原文区间，再看第一场哪条主张的 anchor 与之重叠/包含相同 quote。

这个有点复杂。但更简单、更稳、更容易讲清楚的方案：**对齐靠「改写前引文 after 与 before 的文本关系」+ 归一化引文匹配**。

实际上再想想：我们真正要对比的是「同一份简历改完之后，哪些话还站不住 / 哪些从站不住变成站得住」。对齐的粒度不必到字符级——可以做到「主张文本相似度」或者「锚点 quote 归一化后相等或互相包含」。

但最诚实、最可复现的方法是：**用血缘（lineage）+ 坐标映射**。

让我看看 revise.ts 里的 applyRevisions 和 movedSpans 实现，判断能否加一个「inverseMap」函数把改写后坐标映射回原文坐标。

Let me read revise.ts.

Let me gather what's needed:
- C1: 简历版本对比 — run again on the revised resume and compare verdicts across versions.
- Need: a way to link sessions into a "version chain" — same resume lineage. Design: when you adopt revisions, you can "用改后的简历再跑一遍" — create a new session with the revised text, and record `meta.parentSessionId` / `meta.versionOf`. Then a comparison endpoint compares two sessions' claims by... claim ids restart per session, so comparison must be by category + text similarity or by anchor mapping.

Hmm, key design issue: how to compare claim verdicts across versions? Claims are re-extracted, so C1 in v1 and C1 in v2 aren't the same claim. Options:
1. Match claims by text similarity (normalized overlap) — fragile.
2. Match by category + ordinal — fragile.
3. Compare aggregate: verdict distribution overall and per category (reuse shortfallPlan), plus per-claim diff where text matches.

Best: compute an aggregated comparison (counts by verdict kind, weak count, per-category shortfall) which is robust, plus a "matched pairs" list where claims match by normalized anchor quote or text similarity above threshold, and show those as "这条改完了，判定从 X 变成 Y".

In demo-seed, current 9 combos (3 difficulty × 3 style). Adding a second session per combo would double cassettes. That's a lot. 

Hmm, but actually let me reconsider: cassettes are addressed by prompt digest. The second session has different resume text → different probe/answer/verdict/suggest prompts → all new cassettes. That's +9*... Actually current: per combo, probe 24 (across 9 combos? no). Let me re-read: 56 cassettes total: extract 1 / jd-extract 1 / jd-merge 1 / jd-match 1 / probe 24 / answer 10 / verdict 5 / suggest 12 / counter 1.

Interesting: extract 1 — because RESUME is the same across combos, extract prompt is identical. probe 24 and suggest 12 vary by combo (difficulty affects budget → probe prompts; style affects suggest). answer/verdict vary because probe questions differ.

So adding a second "version" session with a revised resume would add roughly: extract 1 (new resume → new prompt), probe ~ up to 24 more, answer ~10, verdict 5, suggest 12, counter 1. That's ~53 more cassettes → total ~109. That doubles repo size of fixtures. Hmm. That's a real cost.

Alternative for demo: only seed the C1 second run for the **default combo** (normal/normal), not all nine. That keeps the increase to ~+9 cassettes or so (one round of extract/probe/answer/verdict/suggest/counter for the default combo = ~1+2-3+3+5+4+1). Actually per single session in default combo: extract 1, probe (3 rounds × 3 interviewers... ) hmm. For 5 claims × 3 rounds... Let's just implement and measure.

Let me define:

```ts
export const COMPARE_DEFAULT_THRESHOLD = 0.5;

export interface ComparableClaim {
  id: string;
  text: string;
  verdict: VerdictKind | null;
}

export function bigramJaccard(a: string, b: string): number

export interface ClaimPair {
  prevId: string;
  nextId: string;
  prevText: string;
  nextText: string;
  prevVerdict: VerdictKind | null;
  nextVerdict: VerdictKind | null;
  similarity: number;
  shift: VerdictShift;  // 'better' | 'worse' | 'same' | 'unknown'
  rationale: string;    // 中文一句话
}

export type VerdictShiftNote = 'better' | 'worse' | 'same' | 'unknown';

export interface CompareSummary {
  prevTotal, nextTotal, matched, added, removed,
  better, worse, same, unresolved,
  prevDistribution, nextDistribution,
  headline: string
}

export interface SessionComparison {
  threshold: number;
  pairs: ClaimPair[];
  added: ComparableClaim[];
  removed: ComparableClaim[];
  summary: CompareSummary;
  nextInheritedFrom?...
}
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

I have the style conventions. Now let me write packages/shared/src/compare.ts.

Design of compare.ts:

```ts
/**
 * 简历版本对比（P11 · C1）：改完之后再跑一遍，判定到底变了没有。
 */

export interface ClaimLike { id, text, category, verdict: VerdictKind | null }

export interface ClaimPair {
  prevId: string;
  nextId: string;
  prevText: string;
  nextText: string;
  similarity: number;
  prevVerdict: VerdictKind | null;
  nextVerdict: VerdictKind | null;
  shift: ShiftKind;
}

export type ShiftKind = 'better' | 'same' | 'worse' | 'new' | 'gone';
```

Hmm — p11-review says `alignClaims(prev, next, threshold)` returns `{pairs, added, removed}` and `verdictShift(prev, next)` returns better/same/worse. Let me keep that: alignment yields pairs (matched), added (next-only), removed (prev-only). And verdictShift for pairs.

Plus `compareClaims(prev, next, options)` → full CompareResult with stats: verdict distribution change, probe count change, lists of improved/worsened.

I need to keep it pure. Also need `DEFAULT_ALIGN_THRESHOLD`.

Bigram Jaccard: for Chinese text, use character bigrams. For text shorter than 2 chars, use the chars themselves.

Function `bigrams(text)`: strip whitespace and punctuation? Probably normalize whitespace, and it's fine.

`similarity(a, b)` = |A∩B| / |A∪B| using Set of bigrams (multiset vs set — Jaccard on sets is standard).

alignClaims: greedy one-to-one: compute all pairs above threshold, sort by similarity desc, then tie-break by prev index / next index for determinism, take greedily while both unused.

verdictShift: use WEAKNESS_RANK from shortfall.ts to compare: next rank < prev rank → better; equal → same; greater → worse. Handle nulls: if either null → 'same'? Actually a null verdict means unjudged. Let me make shift type include 'unknown' when either side null. Hmm, p11-review says "变好 / 没变 / 变差". I'll add 'unjudged' for when either side has no verdict — better to be explicit. Let's have SHIFT_LABEL: better 变好, same 没变, worse 变差, unjudged 没判定.

Then `compareRuns(prev, next, options)`:
- prev/next: { claims: ClaimLike[], probeCount?: number }
Compute alignment, pairs with shifts, added/removed.
Stats: counts per verdict for prev and next; improved list (pairs where shift==='better'); worsened list.

Let me write a clean module. Also I need a `CompareSummary` headline: e.g. "比上次：站得住的从 1 条变 3 条，新增 2 条主张，追问 18 次 → 15 次".

Let me write it.

Also need to handle: pairs sorted by next index (display order) — the report shows in the new resume's order. I'll sort pairs by nextIndex.

Type for input: since prev/next claims come from DB rows, use `JudgedClaimLike`-compatible plus maybe probes. I'll define:

```ts
export interface CompareClaimLike {
  id: string;
  text: string;
  category: ClaimCategory;
  verdict: VerdictKind | null;
}
```
Actually JudgedClaimLike already is exactly that. Reuse it! Import from shortfall.ts. Good — reuse avoids a second definition. But alignClaims needs text; yes it has text.

So:

```ts
import type { JudgedClaimLike } from './shortfall.ts';
import { WEAKNESS_RANK } from './shortfall.ts';
import type { VerdictKind } from './domain.ts';
import { VERDICT_LABEL } from './domain.ts';
```

Functions:
- `bigramsOf(text: string): Set<string>`
- `textSimilarity(a: string, b: string): number` (0..1, rounded to 4 decimals for stable output)
- `DEFAULT_ALIGN_THRESHOLD = 0.34`
- `alignClaims(prev, next, threshold = DEFAULT_ALIGN_THRESHOLD): ClaimAlignment { pairs: MatchedPair[]; added: next claims; removed: prev claims }`
  where MatchedPair { prev, next, similarity }

```ts
export interface RunSnapshot {
  claims: readonly JudgedClaimLike[];
  /** 这一场问出去多少次追问；没有就是不知道 */
  probes?: number;
}

export interface ClaimComparison {
  threshold: number;
  pairs: ComparedPair[];
  added: JudgedClaimLike[];
  removed: JudgedClaimLike[];
  prevCounts: VerdictCounts;
  nextCounts: VerdictCounts;
  improved: ComparedPair[];
  worsened: ComparedPair[];
  probesBefore: number | null;
  probesAfter: number | null;
  headline: string;
}

export function compareRuns(prev: RunSnapshot, next: RunSnapshot, options: {threshold?: number} = {}): ClaimComparison
```

```ts
 * 全是纯函数、不调模型：对比结果必须能在离线回放里一模一样地复现，
```
